{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"corks","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The dead protective tissue between the bark and cambium in woody plants, with suberin deposits making it impervious to gasses and water."],"synonyms":[{"word":"phellem"}],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["biology","botany","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["The dead protective tissue between the bark and cambium in woody plants, with suberin deposits making it impervious to gasses and water.","The phellem of the cork oak, used for making bottle stoppers, flotation devices, and insulation material."],"synonyms":[{"word":"phellem"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["biology","botany","natural-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Snobs feel it's hard to call it wine with a straight face when the cork is made of plastic.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A bottle stopper made from this or any other material."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An angling float, also traditionally made of oak cork."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The cork oak, Quercus suber."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/kɔɹk/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/kɔːk/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":29843,"upstream_offset":199867712,"upstream_record_sha256":"15f9869b759e344d5c2405efecd35c61e474c1d27003d2cf0addb1ce782f42b3","word":"cork"}

{"forms":[{"form":"humours","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"humor","tags":["alternative","US"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"She has a great sense of humour, and I always laugh a lot whenever we get together.","type":"example"},{"text":"The sensitive subject was treated with humour, but in such way that no one was offended.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The quality of being amusing, comical, funny."],"synonyms":[{"word":"amusingness"},{"word":"comedy"},{"word":"comicality"},{"word":"wit"}],"tags":["UK","uncountable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was in a particularly vile humour that afternoon.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A mood, especially a bad mood; a temporary state of mind or disposition brought upon by an event; an abrupt illogical inclination or whim."],"synonyms":[{"word":"mood"}],"tags":["UK","uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["Any of the fluids in an animal body, especially the four \"cardinal humours\" of blood, yellow bile, black bile and phlegm that were believed to control the health and mood of the human body."],"synonyms":[{"word":"bodily fluid"}],"tags":["UK","archaic","historical","uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["Either of the two regions of liquid within the eyeball, the aqueous humour and vitreous humour."],"tags":["UK","uncountable","usually"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Moist vapour, moisture."],"tags":["UK","obsolete","uncountable","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈhjuː.mə(ɹ)/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhjuːmɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈjuːmɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"something funny","word":"comedy"},{"sense":"something funny","word":"wit"},{"sense":"something funny","word":"witticism"}],"upstream_line":156559,"upstream_offset":719311519,"upstream_record_sha256":"163f3cab60c24a41880e7a3d0031580c35ff664fd166e04c1ad1312142f9709f","word":"humour"}
